The new sports complex ______ will accommodate an Olympic-sized swimming pool and others including fitness center, and a spa, to name just a few.
A.suppliesB.facilitiesC.categoriesD.qualities
A.suppliesB.facilitiesC.categoriesD.qualities

Câu hỏi 27.________ warfare duties primarily to males’ was imperative when combat was hand-to-hand.A.AssigningB.AssignedC.They who assignedD.That they were assigning

Câu hỏi 27.________ warfare duties primarily to males’ was imperative when combat was hand-to-hand.

A.AssigningB.AssignedC.They who assignedD.That they were assigning
A.AssigningB.AssignedC.They who assignedD.That they were assigning
D.That they were assigning
That they were assigning
